### S3-T14 — Public context cards of the self-serve sources
- Decision 0038 named sub-step — **STL/MSTL residual flags** (inventory 18): step one audits S, F as defined in the decision; stop and record why if a blocking fact fails. Baseline: Existing seasonal/context display for C36. Pass/evidence: Audit actual series lengths; define baseline comparison and robust-residual pass criterion before run, as the evaluation supplies neither threshold nor comparator. S6-T03 and S7-T04 reuse a passing method. A pass follows decision 0036 item 2; otherwise retain baseline and record results. Existing dependencies and gate remain; fold-in only, no new task.
- Goal: Build the cards of night-time radiance by area and month (C36), the relative busyness of named venues (C37) and population by age and sex over an area (C38), each a Public fact with its source chain.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] night-time radiance is a Public fact with evidence status Observed and is worded as night-time radiance and never as a measure of business activity (caveat key `night_lights_not_activity`); [ ] a frame-cell value is the mean of the kept pixel values whose centre lies in the cell, and a value for an area is the mean of the frame-cell values whose centres lie inside it, each cell counted once, never a sum and never apportioned (no `apportioned_from`, no `grid_cells_summed`), with `NO_DATA` rather than zero where no value exists and the MTH-109 fixture regression passing; [ ] the relative busyness by hour of a named venue is shown as a Public fact of its `place_public` subject with evidence status Observed, labelled "BestTime relative busyness" with its retrieval date and the provider's update date, worded as a relative index and never as a count of people (caveat key `relative_busyness_not_people`), never shares a component with an estimated-people figure, and shows no figure with its reason for a venue for which the provider publishes none; [ ] population by age and sex over an area the user chose is the sum of whole cells of `pub_pop_agesex_grid`, a cell counting when its centre lies inside and no cell split, shown with its source and year and the caveat key `grid_cells_summed`, is a Public fact with evidence status Observed, and is not an apportionment; [ ] that sum is display only: it is never a criterion of a ranking, never combined with a protected or Simulated figure and never on one chart with one; [ ] every figure comes from `eii_core.calc` or a fixed read function and renders only as a Fact chip; [ ] the claims, components, fragments and caveat keys of the families `night_lights`, `venue_busyness` and `population_age_sex` exist in the closed catalogues; [ ] both languages pass visual tests. [ ] cards open the answer, the comparison and the evidence and method levels through shared fact objects and dated sources; a withheld fixture shows its reason, never zero, with a planted withheld fact rendered as zero that fails; every control works by keyboard and by a single-pointer non-drag action in both languages ([decision 0042](docs/decisions/0042-the-front-end-foundation.md) item 7).
- Scope: calculation cards C36 to C38, their components, catalogue entries and tests.
- Depends: S3-T04, S3-T07, S3-T12, S2-T07, S2-T14, S2-T16.
- Requirements: PUB-90, DAT-141, PUB-29, PRV-122, FE-125, AI-114, MTH-109, MTH-110, MTH-111, PUB-99, PUB-100, PUB-101, AI-125, AI-126.
- Delivery: hard; personas Frontend; status queued.
### S3-T16 — All of Qatar in 3D, with a clock, and the map modes
- Goal: Show the buildings of all of Qatar in 3D with the heights of S2-T20 and a clock (sunlight with shadows on the buildings in view wherever a height exists, under a measured building cap ([decision 0048](docs/decisions/0048-the-shadow-clock-serves-every-area.md)), the map's light and sky, night lights at dusk, a time scrubber and a before-and-after swipe), and the switch of the three map modes with the satellite mode and the owner's capped photorealistic 3D mode ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) items A2 and A7). If the look's mode-switch task (S3-T15) enters this file first, the switch and the modes of item A7 move to it.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] buildings are extruded only to their own height from S2-T20, the legend states that heights are estimates and the error of each fallback opens behind "More evidence", and a planted extrusion by a data value fails (FE-50, FE-52); [ ] sunlight and shadows use deck.gl's lighting effect with its sun light, read first from the current official documentation (rule 42), with its experimental status recorded; shadows are drawn for the buildings in view wherever a height exists, and a planted building without a height that casts a shadow fails; the clock's one setting is its day basis, defaulting to today, and no focus-district setting exists; over the building cap the buildings nearest the view centre keep their shadows and the view says in words that shadows are limited at this zoom, and a planted view over the cap that draws more shadows than the cap, or omits the words, fails; the shadow layer says "estimated heights" in words, with each building's height source and measured error behind "More evidence", and a planted shadow layer without the words fails ([decision 0048](docs/decisions/0048-the-shadow-clock-serves-every-area.md) items 1 to 4); [ ] the scrubber moves only the sun, the light and the sky; no figure changes with it, and a planted hourly figure that changes with the scrubber outside the stored hours of FE-106 fails; [ ] night lights at dusk are drawn from `pub_night_lights` for its own month, worded as night-time radiance with the NASA acknowledgement, never as activity; [ ] the before-and-after swipe shows two states of the user's scenario from tested code or two fixed views of the imagery history of S2-T19, and a planted numeral outside a Fact chip fails; [ ] under reduced motion the clock does not animate; [ ] the frame-rate configuration of slice S1 is repeated with all of Qatar in 3D and with shadows on, the result recorded and the building cap fixed from it; the building cap and the other effects scale to the device's measured frame rate, so a phone is never frozen by a desktop-grade scene; [ ] the open map is the default mode and the only mode that needs no network; the satellite mode is offered only with `ESRI_API_KEY` present and the presenter's offline control not set, its tiles come through the local server's own paths, the browser never holds the key, no tile is stored, and the satellite credits show only inside the collapsed map credits while that mode is on. The scan of the built bundle and of the rendered page judges the map's credit by the place rule of TST-263; [ ] tests and continuous integration never call the imagery provider, and a planted request to the provider's hosts without the key fails; [ ] the photorealistic 3D mode is reached through the same switch and offered only with `MAPTILE_API_KEY` present and the presenter's offline control not set; every request of the mode goes only to the server's own same-origin route, which reads the key at run time from the environment and adds it to each tile request, so the browser never holds the key, and the key is never printed, logged or committed and never reaches a worker (OPS-74, FE-148); [ ] the provider's price per billable request is read again here from its current official page, and the limit of billable requests (3,333 at USD 6.00 per 1,000 requests) is lowered if the price is higher, so that the spend stays within the owner's cap of USD 20 in total; every billable request is counted project-wide and free use is ignored; where the count is kept is settled and recorded here, so that no command, restart, store switch, new release or restore resets or lowers it; and a planted request at the limit is refused, with the mode shown disabled with its reason and the open map kept (OPS-74); [ ] one view loads at most 600 tiles, and a planted view that requests more fails; the mode is never mixed with another basemap in one view and shows the attribution its tiles carry; no tile is cached by the page or stored by the server, and a planted stored tile fails; [ ] tests and continuous integration never call the provider of the photorealistic 3D mode: a hand-made fixture serves its route in tests, and the refusal at the limit and the view budget are tested without the provider; the open map stays the default, and the guided story, every test and every gate pass with the open map alone and with neither key; [ ] `ESRI_API_KEY` and `MAPTILE_API_KEY` are added with empty values to `.env.example` and to the pinned names of `scripts/check_tree.py` (ARC-98); [ ] the 3D-GloBFP credit "Che et al. 2024" and the recorded WSF 3D wording show while buildings with a fallback height from them are drawn, and the GIS centre's source line while an imagery-history view is on (PUB-110). [ ] a swipe or comparison mode keeps the baseline visible and offers an immediate reset, with a planted mode that hides the baseline and a planted mode without the reset that each fail; every drag, scrubber and swipe has a single-pointer non-drag alternative; the right-to-left text plugin condition of S3-T10 holds ([decision 0042](docs/decisions/0042-the-front-end-foundation.md) item 7).
- Scope: the 3D buildings and clock components, the map-mode switch, the satellite mode's tile pass-through, the photorealistic 3D mode's same-origin route with its project-wide count and view budget, the layer registry entries, the two names added to `.env.example` and `scripts/check_tree.py`, and their tests under `web/` and the API.
- Depends: S2-T20, S3-T12, S2-T14, S2-T19.
- Requirements: BRF-97, DES-146, FE-50, FE-52, FE-56, FE-137, FE-139, FE-140, FE-141, FE-143, FE-148, DES-206, DES-207, PUB-100, PUB-106, PUB-109, PUB-110, ARC-35, ARC-36, ARC-98, OPS-73, OPS-74, BE-135, BE-137, TST-248.
- Amendment (decision 0049): its controls hold at the eight named sizes; the building cap and other effects scale to the device's measured frame rate.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 7): [ ] the photorealistic 3D mode takes the spend meter in place of the per-view budget: a running total of billable requests and of USD, a warning at 80 percent of the ceiling until one showing is measured, and no lowered detail; the criterion "one view loads at most 600 tiles" is withdrawn, and the planted refusal at the project-wide hard stop stays; the micro-pass measures a full showing of Doha and of all Qatar, and the ceiling stays the owner's USD 20 (3,333 billable requests at USD 6.00 per 1,000, lowered if the price is higher) until the owner names another (OI-306); this task amends TST-255, BE-139, OPS-74 and the rows of documents 08, 12 and 13 that state the view budget in its own change.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 25): [ ] the fallback panel of building heights is built.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 30): [ ] the before-and-after swipe offers a free choice of imagery dates once S2-T19's amendment of item 30 is met.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 47): [ ] the building cap of the shadows is set from the frame rate measured on the owner's showing machine, so that a full showing draws every shadow in view; the scaling and its words stay for weaker devices.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 48): [ ] the map is drawn at the full pixel ratio on the owner's showing screen when the measured frame rate allows it (OI-121).
- Delivery: hard; personas Frontend; status queued.

### S3-T19 — Script the standing audience-origin check

## slice S4 — Synthetic world, months, presence and traffic

These coarse tasks are refined at the slice S3 gate without weakening their criteria.

### S4-T01 — Contracts and conformance

- Scope: `contracts/`, conformance modules and fixtures.

### S4-T02 — Register and employment world
- Goal: Generate the synthetic employment world and attach it to the register companies by their registration number ([decision 0022](docs/decisions/0022-real-register-companies-with-a-synthetic-world.md)), from public cache inputs, the register files read through `EII_REGISTER_FILE` and `EII_FC_REGISTER_FILE` and the aggregate calibration tables of the owner's notes (decision 0036); whether openings and closures by month stay is open (document 05 Q12); whether the real employment records carry the deployed flag of `worksite_basis` is open (document 05 Q20) and is settled in this task before the flag is generated (owner named by task S1-T10).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] establishment, legal-entity, employer-group and person identifiers use their pinned forms inside the vault (whether the establishment, legal-entity and employer-group forms stay beside the registration number is open, document 05 Q13); [ ] each establishment is a register company, and the attached records join it by its registration number only; [ ] no attached record carries a public place or gazetteer key, name or coordinates; [ ] no company name is generated, and the labels of the two company seats and the four cases have zero collisions with the register's company names and the other real names; [ ] geography comes from the slice S2 frame; [ ] sandbox proof passes; [ ] the totals of the attached records by activity, size band, occupation group and salary level follow the first calibration table’s totals exactly; further aggregate shapes follow the stated checks of decision 0036 and SYN-91, and firm counts and density come from the register; [ ] no registration number, register row or value of the calibration table enters the repository, a fixture, a brief or a log, and tests use the invented stand-in register; [ ] where openings and closures by month stay, they fall in months of the reference period; [ ] only employment gender and released statistics permitted by decision 0037 carry gender; no other sex or gender field or proxy is derived; nationality appears only as the nationality group of the worker records, in the format of the real records, and as the establishment nationality that the register publishes for a company (DAT-141; SYN-13).
- Scope: `custodian/worldgen/register/`, `custodian/worldgen/employment/` and tests.
- Depends: S4-T01, S2-T02.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item 12, the data rule): [ ] no opening or closure month is generated for a register company (the register's own dates and monthly snapshots replace them); legal entities and employer groups are the main-and-branch structure the register shows, otherwise each registration is its own legal entity and group, and planted hidden groups exist only in the invented stand-in register of the tests; the vault keeps the `SYN-E-` and `SYN-L-` identifiers beside the registration number, and `group_id` (`SYN-G-`) is null for every register company and set only in that stand-in register (document 05 Q13, settled; document 03 section 6.1 `business_register.v1`); the registered-address type is the register's own field where shown, otherwise absent; no employment outside the private sector, no deployed worksite and no attribute the confirmed sources do not plausibly hold is generated; a company's attached records hold spells only from its registration month to its cancellation month, and a company with no date shown is present in every month, with the count of such companies reported (SYN-72); a planted generated open or close month, a planted spell before the registration month or after the cancellation month, a planted group that links registrations the register does not link and a planted non-private employer sector each fail (TST-245, DAT-164).
- Requirements: SYN-01, SYN-10, SYN-13, SYN-15, SYN-16, SYN-20, SYN-21, BRF-31, SYN-12, SYN-72, SYN-73, SYN-74.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 33): [ ] the world uses the restored register snapshot of S2-T04 part B: the financial-centre companies, the rows without a registration number and the restored fields.
- Delivery: hard; personas Simulation; status queued.
### S4-T03 — Wages, residence and linkage
- Goal: Generate monthly wages and residence for every month of the reference period for the workers attached to the register companies ([decision 0022](docs/decisions/0022-real-register-companies-with-a-synthetic-world.md)), then deduplicate and link without ambiguous legal-entity joins.

**Decision 0038 spike protocol.** Step one audits blocking facts (G: register geocodes; D: issue/cancellation dates; R: road network/speed profile; S: real-series number/length; F: go-live fields/volumes/keys). A failed audit stops that spike with its reason. Settle missing measurement contracts before execution, never guess them. Failures retain the baseline and record measured results. A pass follows decision 0036's same pipeline, invented real-shaped swap fixture, go-live re-fit/re-backtest, own interval/error procedure, fixed seeds, pinned versions/retreat pins and CPU fallback, with rule 25 and decision 0035 display honesty. Gates, controls and reviews remain. No capability is claimed built.

### S4-T18 — CPU routing and deeper traffic assignment

- Acceptance criteria: [ ] step one audits R, G, F, stopping and recording why if a blocking fact fails; [ ] baseline: Existing CPU routing and ASSUMPTION 11's one-step all-or-nothing assignment. [ ] Time a matrix and an isochrone on the Qatar extract. Compare iterative or multi-class assignment with the one-step baseline, parameters fixed before comparison, no tuning to published congestion. Prove byte-identical output over two builds and across worker counts. The evaluation supplies no routing-time or assignment-improvement threshold: record it for lead review before execution; no pass without it. [ ] failure keeps baseline and records measured results; a pass satisfies the common spike protocol above.
- Scope: CPU routing and assignment spike, baseline comparison and method evidence; S5-T09 consumes only an approved passing method.
- Depends: S1-T11, S4-T08; fact-audit preparation after the S3 gate.
- Requirements: decision 0038 items 1 to 9 and Accepted spike inventory; decision 0036 item 2; AGENTS.md rules 10, 12, 16 to 18, 25 and 42.
- Delivery: hard; builder persona principal methods architect (privacy engineer for S5); strict reviewer persona independent statistical/privacy reviewer from another maker; status queued.

**slice S4 gate.** Every feed passes conformance; fidelity is within tolerances written before the run; identifier-format and name collisions are zero; two builds from one seed are byte-identical; the sandbox proof shows that only public inputs and the aggregate calibration tables of the owner's notes (decision 0036) were read ([decision 0022](docs/decisions/0022-real-register-companies-with-a-synthetic-world.md) item 2); hourly presence exists for every dial situation, the summer switch and the three event days, and hourly flows exist for traffic; the simulated visitor season matches the public museum-visit shape within its tolerance written before the run; and the hourly shape by place category matches the pooled relative busyness within a tolerance written before the run, no venue's level or own shape enters a generator, the weather constants are manifest entries with ranges and a sweep; no worker, residence or company record carries sex or age; the movement splits of DAT-169 and the permitted nationality fields of DAT-141 pass the schema tests of TST-208, and the worker and record-key cases of TST-261 and TST-262 fail as planted.

## slice S5 — Privacy engine and release

These coarse tasks are refined at the slice S4 gate without weakening their criteria.

**Entry condition.** OI-37 is closed: a maker other than the one that last edited the privacy document has reviewed the lead's last ten corrections to it (made on 2026-11-11 after the second confirmation review), and the review is recorded in `PROGRESS.md`. slice S5 does not start before that evidence exists.

### S5-T01 — Partition family and rule sets
- Goal: Evaluate both proposed parameter sets on the one-window, parent-linked partition model, whose window holds several monthly periods.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] OI-37 is closed by a different-maker review of the lead's last ten corrections to it (made on 2026-11-11 after the second confirmation review) and the review is recorded in `PROGRESS.md` before work starts; [ ] roots are resolution-7 parents intersected with municipalities; [ ] a whole-cell unit is one base cell or a union of whole base cells inside one root; [ ] the three cross-root municipality exceptions are the remainder (the union of the failed roots of one municipality), the terminal `ha_home` unit (the union of every residence unit and remainder of one municipality) and the municipality unit of each of the four pay partitions `rp_all_money`, `rp_all_quartile`, `wp_all_money` and `wp_all_quartile` (the union of that partition's units and remainders in one municipality, holding exact sums of its published units' treated leaves apart from the two unreleased stayer wage bills behind its stayer index, PRV-16, PRV-55); [ ] one parent-linked partition family exists per contributor universe and independent merges never cross; [ ] `home_area_share` belongs to that family and no row is published when either municipality-side complement is below the active count minimum; [ ] all-or-nothing siblings hold; [ ] remainders follow policy; [ ] version 1 builds no second window or cross-window test; the mover canary and adjacent-month predicates remain in the version 1 gate; the window's protected `period` spans its monthly periods, and differencing across those months is attacked in S5-T03.
- Scope: `custodian/privacy/partition/`, rules files and property tests.
- Depends: S4-T05.
- Requirements: PRV-10, PRV-24, PRV-53, TST-20.
- Delivery: hard; personas Privacy; status queued.
### S5-T02 — Stable treated leaf atoms
- Goal: Treat only mutually exclusive leaf atoms and derive every within-family total as the exact sum of that family's treated leaves. No cross-family equality is required.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] cell keys are computed over distinct contributors; [ ] `other` is a treated leaf where pooling occurred; [ ] every stored total inside one additive family is the exact sum of that family's treated leaves, and no equality is asserted across families; [ ] the minimum-group, largest-group and top-two tests apply to every band and category atom; [ ] removing any one employer group leaves every published atom at or above the active count minimum; [ ] controlled rounding preserves exact within-family margins and a negative residual withholds the split; [ ] the pinned row-dropping order, fixed test order and reason-code mapping are implemented exactly; [ ] the privacy worked example reproduces its pinned result; [ ] identical inputs repeat exactly; [ ] one treatment holds for every month of the window; [ ] keys never cross the release boundary.
- Scope: privacy treatment modules and primitive release builders.
- Depends: S5-T01.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item 12): [ ] the employer group of the dominance and removal tests is the register's main-and-branch structure, or each registration alone; every dominance rule, the removal test and their planted tests are unchanged and run on these groups.
- Requirements: PRV-24, PRV-29, PRV-35, PRV-42, PRV-44, PRV-45, PRV-46, PRV-110, TST-24, TST-26.
- Delivery: hard; personas Privacy; status queued.

- Acceptance criteria: [ ] presence counts are released only at release-unit grain, under the panel minimum, complementary suppression and rounding of document 06; [ ] each count carries its range and its group (workers, residents or visitors); [ ] every dial situation, the summer switch and the three event days are released; [ ] link speeds by hour come from released flows assigned to the routing graph with the one fixed volume-delay rule of S1-T11, never from protected rows; [ ] no count or speed is a fact about a named place; [ ] no pay-band table is added to the release and no dimension to the presence key (PRV-34, PRV-58, PRV-126); [ ] parity covers the new tables.
- Scope: presence and flow release builders, the assignment step and their tests.

- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item 16): [ ] the release holds the split tables of DAT-169 for presence and flow; values below the movement family's panel minimum are pooled into `other` under the order and tie-break of PRV-35 until the pooled value passes or the whole split kind is withheld for the cell; a planted recoverable value fails (TST-260); a planted movement output with a person-level row or a record key fails (TST-261); a split value under the minimum panel devices of the active parameter set (20 under set A, 30 under set B) of a kept cell contributes to a publishable `other` and stays in the released split's share denominator, and no released value is below the family's minimum (TST-260).
- Requirements: PRV-57, PRV-58, PRV-60, SYN-54, TST-40, PRV-124, DAT-138, DAT-139.

### S5-T03 — Closure and adversarial audits
- Decision 0038 named sub-step — **question-factory seed rows** (inventory 7): step one audits F, S; G, D, R as relevant as defined in the decision; stop and record why if a blocking fact fails. Baseline: Existing attack corpus. Pass/evidence: Add seed questions for reconstruction, differencing, averaging, forbidden joins and source-grain violations as rows of existing tests; retain all attack sizes and controls-off/on requirements. No dependency on S8-T12 and no new gate. A pass follows decision 0036 item 2; otherwise retain baseline and record results. Existing dependencies and gate remain; fold-in only, no new task.
- Goal: Run exhaustive structural closure checks and the one attacker-view acceptance model, plus the typed attack suite, under both parameter sets. The audit is evidence under a stated attacker and is not called a proof.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] exhaustive checks cover every unit, every pair of protected partitions and every area `make_area` can produce after pin reduction, and a drawn shape or free radius is covered by the proof of PRV-111 that any set of published whole-cell units maps to one home family's leaves; [ ] at least 10,000 seeded fuzz pairs pass per release and per parameter set as regression evidence; [ ] the one acceptance model fixes every published leaf to its untreated aggregate, and no released-value interval is used for acceptance; [ ] every signed knowledge fixture is compared with sealed truth before optimisation, and any disagreement fails the build; [ ] published-means-passed constraints are present for every published leaf; [ ] targets the model cannot tell apart form one target class and are audited once; [ ] a time budget is set from a measured run at the start of slice S5 and recorded as a rule, a run over budget fails the build, and the model is not relaxed to meet it; [ ] `artefacts/attacker_view_audit.json` and `artefacts/attacker_view_public_totals.json` are the audit configuration and the public-total inventory; [ ] the audit fails when an applicable public total is unconfigured, and it covers `home_area_share` residuals; [ ] every person's feasible pay interval spans at least two released bands and every employer's headcount and wage-bill residuals exceed the declared bounds; [ ] PRV-121 allows only exact presolve, and one fixture world solved in both the full form and the reduced form gives identical statuses and identical minimum and maximum for every target class; [ ] the model does not represent what the layout tells about a failed population, and the typed attack suite holds one attack for each layout signal (a merged band, a category pooled into `other`, merged siblings, a withheld remainder and a released reason code); [ ] every canary and threat outcome has a typed recovery predicate; [ ] the suite attacks cross-month differencing, apportioning to drawn shapes and linking the register-company list with area figures, with every employee count as attacker knowledge, each with its typed recovery predicate, and runs the two planted cases of [decision 0022](docs/decisions/0022-real-register-companies-with-a-synthetic-world.md) item 7; [ ] the suite treats the figure of people present by pay band (card C32, PRV-126) as a function of observable release fields; [ ] controls off recover every predicate and controls on recover none, for each parameter set; [ ] the mover canary of PRV-75 and the adjacent-month predicates of PRV-116 are in the version 1 gate, no consecutive-window predicate is, and no person-level movement-protection claim is made; [ ] reviewer attacks become permanent; [ ] no sentence in the audit report calls the result a proof.
- Scope: privacy audits, attack suite and signed reports.
- Depends: S5-T02, S5-T09, S5-T10.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item 12): [ ] the hidden-group canary and the planted hidden groups run on the invented stand-in register only, never on groups invented between real register companies; [ ] the acceptance model holds the Public registration and cancellation dates of register companies as attacker knowledge (PRV-113 item 13, PRV-69): a company, listed or not, has no jobs before its registration month or after its cancellation month, the constraint also binds the class variables of a target employer group, a move between two registrations of one employer group that keeps its leaf stays part of a stayer cohort, and a model that omits the constraint fails the audit build; the entrant canary of document 05 section 10 (a register company whose registration month falls in the window, chosen from what the register shows and labelled in the scenario, never planted, and, in the stand-in register of the tests, an invented company that opens in one month of the window) is on the canary list of PRV-75, with its typed recovery predicate for the known-entrant attack on a company's pay (`listed_firm_pay`).
- Requirements: PRV-49, PRV-67, PRV-69, PRV-73, PRV-75, PRV-76, PRV-112, PRV-113, PRV-116, PRV-121, TST-120, TST-220, TST-221, TST-222.
- Delivery: hard; personas Privacy; status queued.

**Decision 0038 spike protocol.** Step one audits blocking facts (G: register geocodes; D: issue/cancellation dates; R: road network/speed profile; S: real-series number/length; F: go-live fields/volumes/keys). A failed audit stops that spike with its reason. Settle missing measurement contracts before execution, never guess them. Failures retain the baseline and record measured results. A pass follows decision 0036's same pipeline, invented real-shaped swap fixture, go-live re-fit/re-backtest, own interval/error procedure, fixed seeds, pinned versions/retreat pins and CPU fallback, with rule 25 and decision 0035 display honesty. Gates, controls and reviews remain. No capability is claimed built.

- Acceptance criteria: [ ] step one audits F, G, S, stopping and recording why if a blocking fact fails; [ ] baseline: Unchanged release controls and controls-off attacks. [ ] Reconstruction, cross-month and drawn-shape differencing and averaging recover zero predicates with controls on; attacks succeed with controls off. Include gender under decision 0037; all attack sizes and tolerances stay unchanged. Compare cell-key consistent perturbation inside the custodian plane only; no second exported treatment. [ ] failure keeps baseline and records measured results; a pass satisfies the common spike protocol above.
- Scope: Custodian-plane attack and perturbation evidence, alongside existing release audits.
- Depends: S4-T01 for audit preparation before S5; OI-37 closure, S5-T02 and S5-T03 for execution during S5.
- Requirements: decision 0038 items 1 to 9 and Accepted spike inventory; decision 0036 item 2; AGENTS.md rules 10, 12, 16 to 18, 25 and 42.
- Delivery: hard; builder persona principal methods architect (privacy engineer for S5); strict reviewer persona independent statistical/privacy reviewer from another maker; status queued.

**slice S5 gate.** Closure is proved by exhaustive structural checks, and a fuzz run of at least 10,000 area pairs per release and per parameter set passes; the attacker-view audit passes with every applicable public total configured; with controls off every typed predicate is recovered, and with controls on none is, for each parameter set, the attacks on cross-month differencing, on apportioning to drawn shapes and on linking the register-company list with area figures included; exactly one protected table set leaves the custodian plane; presence counts and monthly periods leave it only at release-unit grain under the controls of document 06, the one company-level release being the employee count of the register-company list (PRV-125), and traffic link speeds come from released flows only; no table and no presence-key dimension is added for the figure of people present by pay band, which the attack suite covers as a function of observable release fields (PRV-126); the loader rejects any table not in the manifest; and the release hash is reproducible. PRV-138's planted drawn-polygon differencing attack runs with every existing attack under both parameter sets, recovering its target with controls off and nothing with controls on; version 1 still builds no second protected window, including a test-only window.

This orders existing work only: no new data or acceptance-criterion change. See document 14 **Priority before go-live — decision 0070**.

- Amendment ([decision 0045](docs/decisions/0045-eiis-own-capabilities-and-the-snapshot-archive.md) items 3 and 7; capability 4): [ ] visitor opportunity uses only decision 0023 item 16's permitted one-way movement aggregates by nationality group, age band and gender, beside separate Public hotel and venue-busyness components; no roaming-derived origin attribute, nationality proxy, person row or joining key is created, and Public and simulated figures never share one component; [ ] the mandatory limitation sentences say that device panels are not unique visitors, presence is not purchases, and calibration fit is not independent validation; a planted roaming-derived attribute, crossed split, person row, record-joining key, unique-visitor claim, purchase claim or validation claim each fails (AI-138, DAT-169, PRV-134, SYN-89, TST-260, TST-261, TST-262).

### S6-T09 — Income indicators with monthly trends
- Goal: Complete the six income indicators for covered earners with monthly trends: residential income beside presence by time of day, residence and workplace geography, and wage trends by sector and area separated from changes in worker mix (by area per municipality and nationally; by sector national only, from the Public wage series, in S7-T08).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] each indicator shows its trend across the window's monthly periods and what changed; [ ] wage trends are separated from changes in worker mix by tested code; [ ] residential income is set beside presence by time of day and never combined with a presence count into one number, with the one exception of the figure of people present by pay band (card C32, PRV-126), which indicator 4 shows as a third, Inferred element beside its separate facts (S6-T16); [ ] residence and workplace geography shows broad commuting catchments and access gaps only where workplaces are reliably known; [ ] the average is never shown alone; [ ] every trend figure keeps the snap, or the label of its whole municipality or national unit, and the controls of its release table; [ ] the pay figures and the stayer index are shown for a municipality unit that contains units of the area and for the national unit of the `money` and `quartile` pay partitions, each labelled with its own released area, never snapped, averaged or combined, with the municipality earner count from the `money` unit and the national earner count from `rp_all_count` or `wp_all_count` (PRV-16, PRV-55, MTH-55, MTH-76, MTH-78).
- Scope: income-trend methods, cards and their tests.
- Depends: S6-T02, S6-T03, S5-T10, S6-T16.
- Requirements: PRV-41, PRV-54, MTH-21, BRF-18, FE-22, BRF-75, MTH-80, MTH-81, PRV-55, PRV-56, FE-113, PRV-16, PRV-126.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.

### S6-T14 — Forecasts of published series
- Goal: Forecast the published series of the closed list of card C30, and the released monthly statistics of the custodian-type feeds, in `eii_core.calc`, choose each model by backtest and show every forecast with its empirical interval and its measured backtest error.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] a series of the closed, versioned list is forecast only when the public data pack holds its source table (S2-T17), and a series without one is listed as open and returns nothing; [ ] the model of a series is chosen by a rolling-origin backtest over a closed, versioned list of candidate models (at least seasonal naive, drift and exponential smoothing, an `ASSUMPTION` confirmed here) that keeps the candidate with the smallest error by one fixed measure (mean absolute scaled error, an `ASSUMPTION` confirmed here), and no person and no language model chooses a model; [ ] a series too short for the backtest minimum returns `NO_DATA` with the reason key `series_too_short`, shown as "not enough history", never a model without a backtest; [ ] a series that cannot be negative returns `NO_DATA` with the reason key `forecast_below_zero` for a point forecast below zero, an interval end below zero is shown as zero and marked, and a planted regression fails on a negative point, on another reason key, on a clamp without the required mark and on an unclamped interval end; [ ] the interval is empirical, built from the backtest's own errors at each horizon, and its caption states how many past values of the backtest fell inside the range that the other past errors gave, both counts as Fact chips (caveat key `forecast_backtest_range`); [ ] the interval is never called a probability or a confidence level and never said to hold for the next value, a forecast chart draws it as a band with that caption, and the projection of card C21 keeps its own rules and stays a projection; [ ] a forecast is an Inferred fact on the subject and geography of its series, each forecast is computed at request time from release tables and is never written to the release, its lineage names the method and version and the series, the model and the horizon identify the fact, the interval ends, the backtest error and the number of backtest points are facts of the same computation shown with it, and it resolves to the source like any fact (rule 19); [ ] a forecast of a Public split by sex or nationality stays on the source's own geography and categories; [ ] licence issues and licence cancellations are forecast under those names and no net series is formed from them; [ ] a series with an open reconciliation (OI-03) emits no forecast, the property-price forecast shows the deal counts of its history, and a forecast of announced investment by sector is a sector-level Inferred fact that keeps the source's estimate note and is never attached to a project; [ ] the code reads one series contract, which the released series of the custodian-type feeds also meet, and the card also takes the released monthly series of the window: pay level, earner count and wage bill, the stayer index at municipality level, and establishments and employment by area; [ ] such a forecast is a fixed function of rows that are already released, carries the Synthetic class, the word "forecast" and the caveat keys `synthetic_not_evidence_about_qatar`, `synthetic_change` and `forecast_backtest_range`, has the `area` of its series as its subject and the last released month as its origin, and is off in the Public-only mode as a ranking criterion; [ ] a series with a carried-forward month in its window is not forecast and returns the suppression `CARRIED_FORWARD`, as card C21 rules for projections, and a forecast or projection over the released months is not a protected statistic of a later month (PRV-54); [ ] every function is a pure function of the series passed in, and each candidate model and the backtest have a regression test on a short hand-made series whose arithmetic a reader can check by hand; [ ] the card's sentence is the only wording: "Forecast of {series} for {period}: {value}, range {low} to {high}. In the backtest {x} of {y} past values fell inside the range that the other past errors gave; its error was {error}."
- Scope: forecast and backtest modules in `core/eii_core/calc/`, calculation card C30, components and tests.
- Depends: S2-T03, S2-T07, S2-T17.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item 12): [ ] no forecast reads a generated opening or closure; the establishments of an area by month are register companies by their own dates and monthly snapshots, a Public count of DAT-164 and not a released series, so the released monthly series of the criteria are pay level, earner count and wage bill, the stayer index at municipality level and employment by area, which replaces "establishments and employment by area" there; whether a forecast of establishments by area joins the closed list of card C30 as a Public series is fixed here with that list (ARC-138).
- Requirements: MTH-98, MTH-01, DAT-90, PUB-94, TST-54, MTH-99, MTH-100, DAT-158, PUB-103, AI-121.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 38): [ ] card C30 forecasts every listed series for which a Public series exists, the monthly establishments-by-area series among them where one exists, each with its backtest interval and measured error (rule 25).
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.
- Step-one record: the pure calculation/admission/lineage handoff and closed component bindings are carried forward on the current base; the concrete release reader, authenticated seat dispatch and canonical fact emitter remain absent (OI-324). Step two owns the card, stepped band and rendered states on S3-T09's shared Fact family. No criterion is ticked and status remains queued; evidence is in PROGRESS.md under S6-T14 step one.
### S6-T15 — Entry, closure and survival base rates
- Goal: Compute the entry and closure base rates from the Public licence tables, by activity and on the source's own geography, and answer the survival base rate with `NO_DATA`.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] each rate is computed by activity and on the source's own geography, never for a drawn area, and only where the Public tables carry the denominator; elsewhere the answer is `NO_DATA` with the reason key `no_denominator`; [ ] the entry and closure base rates are flows over the published active stock of the same activity and geography, labelled approximate, each with its two counts and period, and the Public-licence survival branch returns `NO_DATA` with the reason key `no_denominator`, because the Public licence tables do not link a cancellation to its issue, until a Public source does (OI-97), so that no survival figure is computed or shown; [ ] each rate shows its numerator, its denominator and its period as Fact chips, and bulk months are handled by the stated rule of the method card once the list of bulk months is confirmed (OI-21); [ ] a base rate is worded as a share of past licences of that activity and area (caveat key `base_rate_not_your_chance`), never as the chance that the user's own venture survives, and the word "odds" is never on screen; [ ] no figure of issues minus cancellations is computed or shown, the screen names stay "licence issues" and "licence cancellations", and no rate is computed from the openings and closures of register companies; [ ] a base rate is a Public fact with evidence status Observed, the status of the churn rate of card C2 (fixed arithmetic over published counts, caveat `calculated`), and it carries the caveat key `base_rate_not_your_chance`; its lineage names the method, the numerator, the denominator and the period and resolves to the source; [ ] every function is a pure function of the counts passed in, with a regression test whose arithmetic a reader can check by hand.
- Amendment (decision 0036): the existing Public-licence branch retains its missing-cohort refusal; S6-T23 and S6-T24 add source-general survival and entry methods under MTH-123. Generated provenance is not itself a failing test.
- Scope: base-rate methods (calculation card C31), components and tests.
- Depends: S2-T03, S2-T07.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item 12): [ ] no rate is computed from the openings and closures of register companies (DAT-164), which replaces the Synthetic births and closures named in the criteria above.
- Requirements: MTH-19, MTH-20, TST-54, PUB-94, BRF-62, MTH-101, MTH-102, DAT-159, PUB-104, AI-122.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 35): [ ] closures come from the cancellations table; the three bed-linen trade lines enter the import baskets only if the basket allows them and OI-171's condition holds.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 39): [ ] part B delivers entry and closure base rates in full in both languages, with the screen, claims beyond the municipality where the source's own geography gives them, Arabic templates with no English inside, and the three caveats registered; survival stays `NO_DATA` until a Public source is recorded (OI-97).
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.
### S6-T16 — People present by pay band
- Goal: For a whole release unit, a day type and an hour, show the simulated workers and residents present split by pay band, as a fixed function of published rows.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] `eii_core.calc` computes the figure in the product plane from published rows only: the published presence groups of the unit's cells, and the published pay-band shares of the same unit (the worker-pay partition for workers present, the resident-pay partition for residents present); [ ] the figure is returned only when every row it draws on is published, a withheld, suppressed, carried-forward or remainder row is never filled in, and the figure passes through the suppression reason of the row that is missing; [ ] visitors have no pay band and are shown as one count without a band; the band shares are those of the unit's covered earners, so the figure does not separate people who have no wage record: it assumes that the workers and residents present have the pay bands of the unit's covered workers and residents, and its caveat says so; [ ] the figure is never apportioned to a drawn outline, and both sides use the snapped unit list of PRV-18; [ ] it is Inferred and carries the backend origin marker (decision 0035 item 3), never rendered, with the caveat key `present_by_pay_band_assumed`, and it is worded as estimated people around this location by pay band, never as spending, spending power, income present or purchasing power; [ ] no table is added to the release and no dimension to the presence key (PRV-34, PRV-58), mobility still meets wage statistics only area to area (PRV-99), and every other number that combines a protected value with a presence, flow or traffic value stays forbidden; [ ] a planted fixture with one withheld row returns the suppression reason and no figure.
- Scope: calculation card C32, its components and tests.
- Depends: S6-T01, S6-T03, S5-T09.
- Requirements: PRV-126, PRV-41, PRV-58, PRV-18, MTH-77, MTH-103, MTH-104, DAT-160, AI-124.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.
### S6-T17 — Site demand and revenue estimate
- Goal: For an area or pin that the user chose, give a low, a middle and a high revenue of an outlet's catchment with its main drivers.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] the inputs are the people present by pay band (S6-T16), the competitors from public listings as counts and distances, and category spend ranges calibrated to the Public survey of hotels and restaurants, for the three activities of OI-20 unless the owner records another list; [ ] the model runs a seeded Monte Carlo over the stated ranges of its uncertain parameters, the seed never forms a bare run of exactly eight or eleven digits (rule 9), low, middle and high are fixed percentiles of the model's own draws worded as cases of the model, and the main drivers come from a one-at-a-time swing computed in code; [ ] the percentiles and the parameter ranges are fixed in document 07 and confirmed here (`ASSUMPTION`); [ ] while any input is Simulated or Synthetic the estimate carries the required backend caveat key `estimate_on_simulated_inputs` (backend-only under decision 0035 item 3; never rendered); [ ] it is an Inferred fact whose class is the least-real class of its inputs (Simulated while presence is simulated); its subject is the `area` formed from the area or pin that the user chose, never the pin or a place, it is never an estimate for a named real building or business, and no figure for a named competitor is computed for display, shown, stored or logged; [ ] a pay band is never called spending power, and the spend per person by band is a stated, visible and editable range of the model; [ ] the estimate is worded as an estimate and never as a forecast; [ ] it is off in the Public-only mode and unavailable while the reconciliation of the restaurant totals (OI-03) keeps the survey figures off screen; [ ] every function is a pure function of its inputs and seed, with a regression test whose arithmetic a reader can check by hand.
- Scope: calculation card C33, the estimate tool, components and tests.
- Depends: S6-T16, S6-T10, S3-T05, S2-T03, S2-T07.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item 12): [ ] the share of people present who buy and the spend per purchase are the user's own inputs with no prefill, as card C35 does; no survey benchmark is shown beside them; no spend range is prefilled or calibrated, and a planted prefilled share or spend fails; this replaces the calibrated category spend ranges of the first criterion.
- Requirements: PRV-126, PRV-41, MTH-77, PRV-65, MTH-57, MTH-01, MTH-105, MTH-106, DAT-161, AI-123.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 49): [ ] the site estimate is shown as low, middle and high with its main drivers (rule 25), with cited Public defaults the user can edit where a source exists and the user's own input elsewhere, and never attached to a named building (rule 24), with a planted uncited default refused.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.
### S6-T11 — Best-location ranking
- Decision 0038 named sub-step — **rank acceptability** (inventory 5): step one audits F as defined in the decision; stop and record why if a blocking fact fails. Baseline: Existing visible-weight ranking. Pass/evidence: Match R smaa on a fixed matrix with fixed-seed reproducibility; Pareto and regret views say share of the weight settings you allow, never odds, and retain the rank-stability sentence. A pass follows decision 0036 item 2; otherwise retain baseline and record results. Existing dependencies and gate remain; fold-in only, no new task.
- Goal: Rank candidate areas for an activity with every criterion, its weight and its value visible, weights the user can change, a rank-stability sentence beside every rank and a Public-only mode (former Later item L1).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] ranks are computed by tested code in `eii_core.calc`, never by a model or the browser; [ ] every criterion, its weight and its value are shown, and the weights are labelled a judgement; [ ] the user can change the weights; [ ] a rank-stability sentence stands beside every rank; [ ] a Public-only mode ranks on Public facts only; [ ] a forecast (card C30), a base rate (card C31) and the site estimate (card C33) may be criteria, each visible with its value, its weight and its interval, range or counts, method and source, and each removable by the user, and the rank shows which criteria moved it; [ ] a forecast or base rate of a Public series ranks only candidates on that series' own geography, or enters as an apportioned count where card C18 allows one; [ ] the site estimate, a forecast of a released monthly series (which carries the Synthetic class) and any criterion with a Simulated or Synthetic input are off in the Public-only mode; [ ] no Public split by sex or nationality is a criterion, and no list of register companies is ranked by a protected or Simulated figure other than the employee count shown on its cards, and never by a company's wages or another unreleased labour or residence value (PRV-125, [decision 0022](docs/decisions/0022-real-register-companies-with-a-synthetic-world.md)); [ ] the screen keeps the name "Areas ranked by the criteria you set" and the word "best" stays off screen; [ ] no rank is worded as a verdict, a probability, a confidence claim, a forecast, an estimate or advice to act, and a rank that uses a forecast as a criterion is still not a forecast.
- Scope: ranking method (calculation card C11), ranking tool, components and tests.
- Depends: S6-T03, S6-T09, S6-T10, S6-T14, S6-T15, S6-T17.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) items A3 and 12): [ ] the site estimate enters a ranking only with the user's own share who buy and spend per purchase; with none typed it is not offered as a criterion; [ ] the zoning of a candidate (the permitted uses, height and setbacks of the GIS centre's layer of S2-T19) may be a criterion that the user can see, weight and remove, a returned Public fact on its own geography that is never worded as an approval, a permit or availability, and a planted criterion worded as one fails (BRF-98, FE-153).
- Amendment (decision 0044): [ ] a reviewed clarifying question of the closed question catalogue, answered by the user, changes a visible criterion or weight through a typed catalogue action, and the ranking and the map re-rank by tested code with the rank-stability sentence; a planted weight or criterion value chosen by a model (not by the user's typed answer or the catalogue) fails.
- Amendment (decision 0044): [ ] the lab's parity contract is executed for ranking explanations in both languages; a planted English-only string in a ranking explanation fails.
- Requirements: MTH-01, MTH-45, FE-45, BRF-40, BRF-67, BRF-77, MTH-57, MTH-64, MTH-65, FE-110.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.
### S6-T12 — The reverse and similar areas
- Decision 0038 named sub-step — **Ward stability and Gower neighbours** (inventory 13): step one audits G, F as defined in the decision; stop and record why if a blocking fact fails. Baseline: Existing neighbours and no stable three types. Pass/evidence: Ward k=3 requires bootstrap Jaccard at least 0.75, otherwise no stable three types; specify separate Gower comparison before running; no municipality clustering. A pass follows decision 0036 item 2; otherwise retain baseline and record results. Existing dependencies and gate remain; fold-in only, no new task.

- Depends: S6-T11, S6-T13, S2-T19.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) items A3 and 12): [ ] site fit shows a plot only as "permitted by zoning and looks empty", decided by tested code from Public layers only (a parcel of `gis_parcels` whose use `gis_zoning` permits, with no address point of `gis_address` and no building), with the caveat of section 12 of document 04, never as approved or available; on invented fixture layers an empty permitted parcel takes the label and a parcel with an address point, one with a building and one whose use zoning does not permit do not, and a planted "approved" or "available" wording in English or in Arabic fails (BRF-107, TST-247).
- Amendment ([decision 0045](docs/decisions/0045-eiis-own-capabilities-and-the-snapshot-archive.md) items 5 and 7; capabilities 11 and L5): [ ] the readiness screen combines only Public parcel zoning and empty-footprint evidence, zone electricity and district-cooling context, S6-T13 peak-hour reach, and a recorded bare-land price below the district guide as separate, dated criteria of tested code; electricity context states that 2026 covers January to August only; no electricity or cooling value becomes a connection or capacity claim, no reach or price becomes an approval, and no plot is called available, for sale or vacant; a planted missing criterion, mismatched geography, post-August electricity claim, utility-capacity claim, non-bare-land price, unavailable reach, approved/available/vacant label or recommendation each fails (BRF-98, BRF-107, PUB-105, MTH-46, MTH-116, FE-153, DES-212, TST-247).
- Amendment ([decision 0047](docs/decisions/0047-eiis-own-decision-backlog-and-acceptance-targets.md) item A28): [ ] for a candidate parcel intersecting a Public protected-area class in the dated S2-T19 GIS input, tested code returns a visible blocker beside the existing zoning, imagery, deal and utility evidence; an absent protected-area classification returns `NO_DATA`, and a planted intersecting parcel without a blocker, an inferred availability from missing classification or a blocker based on an undated layer fails.

### S6-T18 — Optional 3D view
- Goal: Build the optional 3D view that extrudes whole release units by one protected area statistic or by the simulated crowd density.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] the view is off by default; [ ] it extrudes whole release units only, by one protected area statistic or by the simulated crowd density, and its marks, suppression and snap rule are those of the flat view; [ ] every extruded value is also readable as a Fact chip in the flat view; [ ] a building keeps its own height and is never extruded by a data value; [ ] only traffic and crowd density animate, and the animation stops under reduced motion; [ ] an extruded layer counts as the one hero layer that may be visible; [ ] the slice S1 frame-rate configuration is repeated with the 3D view on and its result recorded, with deck.gl read from its current official documentation (rule 42); [ ] the claims and components of the family `view_3d` exist in the closed catalogues.
- Scope: 3D view components, the layer registry entry and their tests under `web/`.
- Depends: S6-T01, S6-T03, S6-T08.
- Requirements: DES-50, FE-49, FE-84, TST-132.
- Delivery: hard; personas Frontend; status queued.
### S6-T19 — Optional live traffic provider
- Goal: Build the optional live traffic provider switch: live flow tiles and traffic-aware drive-time areas, off by default (an amendment of card C25).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] `EII_TRAFFIC_PROVIDER` is `simulated` by default, which gives the simulated congestion by hour as before; [ ] with `tomtom`, `TOMTOM_API_KEY` present and the presenter's offline control not set, the API process fetches live flow tiles and traffic-aware drive-time areas from the provider and passes them on, the browser never holds the key and still contacts only its own origin, and no provider host is held by the built JavaScript; [ ] the key is read from the environment only and is never printed, logged, committed or put into a work tree, a brief or a sandbox; [ ] nothing of a response is written to disk, to the release, to the ledger or to a log, and the run-time lineage keeps the request, the retrieval time and the response hash; [ ] a live tile is a picture layer with the attribution "© TomTom" visible while the layer is on, held as configured text, and no numeral is read from a tile (caveat key `live_traffic_provider`); [ ] a traffic-aware drive-time area enters through the adapter as an area of the session, bound to the session and seat like a drawn shape and marked with the provider and its retrieval time, protected figures over it still snap to whole release units, and a figure over it carries the least-real class of its inputs and is repeatable for that stored area; [ ] with the default provider, traffic is computed only from released values and the same question returns the same numbers, and the invariance test applies to the default; [ ] no test, gate, command, preflight or the guided story needs the key or the network, preflight only probes and reports the provider as not configured without a key, and tests use fixtures made by hand in the documented response format with no response of a live service committed; [ ] the provider's rule on storing responses is not verified (OI-99), so nothing is stored.
- Scope: provider adapter, tile pass-through, drive-time-area adapter, the setting `EII_TRAFFIC_PROVIDER`, the attribution component, hand-made fixtures and tests.
- Depends: S6-T07, S6-T08, S3-T13.
- Requirements: ARC-135, ARC-35, OPS-37, PRV-124, PRV-122, BE-117, PUB-95, PUB-96, AI-127.
- Amendment ([decision 0064](docs/decisions/0064-the-narrowing-rows-decided.md) item 2): [ ] the live layer may be shown to others under the provider's paid plan, a spend choice the lead lists for the owner on the subscriptions file (OI-304); until the owner holds it, showing stays the owner's own evaluation on the free plan; off by default, nothing of a response stored, attribution shown, and never needed by a test, gate, command or the guided story (rules 4 and 27).
- Delivery: hard; personas Backend; status queued.
### S6-T20 — Talent available near this site
- Goal: For a site, show "Talent available near this site" within 10, 20 and 30 minutes' drive: the talent pool (residents by occupation and pay band within reach) and the competing demand (jobs of the same occupations within reach) as two released figures side by side, qualified people within reach per competing job within reach by occupation group at those cut-offs (MTH-122), with home-to-work flows drawn as a flow map and animated as aggregate flows ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item A1; journey D5, the map and the guided story).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] the answer is called "Talent available near this site", never "who can work for you", and no talent answer is worded or laid out as a list of people (PRV-128); [ ] for each cut-off of the closed list 10, 20 and 30 minutes, the catchment is a drive-time area of the released road graph from the site, snapped to whole release units like every area before any protected figure is read, with the snap sentence (PRV-18); [ ] the talent pool, the residents by occupation group and pay band within reach (`occupation_pay` on the residence basis), and the competing demand, the jobs of the same occupation groups within reach (`occupation_pay` on the workplace basis), are two released figures side by side, each with its one home family in its lineage (PRV-30), and a third, qualified people within reach per competing job within reach, by occupation group at each cut-off, is the quotient of those two released figures for the same occupation group and cut-off, computed only from the two released tables after the privacy engine's work, shown to one decimal with the label "qualified people per competing job within N minutes", never called competition-adjusted and never an index (MTH-122); no other ratio, difference, share or rank of the talent pool against the competing demand is computed, shown or stored; any other ratio of two protected families outside the closed exceptions of MTH-55, and any ratio computed before release, is refused (PRV-127); a planted ratio computed from records fails (TST-257), a planted ratio with a suppressed input fails (TST-258), the passing case from the two released tables passes (TST-259), and a planted read on units chosen by a travel time instead of the snap and a planted figure on an unsnapped outline each fail (PRV-127); where either input is suppressed or below the minimum count the ratio is not shown and the card says why (reason key `ratio_input_withheld`), and where the competing-jobs figure is zero no ratio is shown and the card says that no competing job is within reach (reason key `no_competing_jobs`); the two-step floating catchment is not used for this ratio and PySAL `access` is neither pinned nor used; [ ] every figure is a group statistic released under document 06, with minimum counts of people and of employers in every cell, the dominance rule, and pay as bands, median and middle range; it reads only released tables, a withheld, suppressed, carried-forward or remainder row is never filled in and its reason passes through, and a planted unreleased or suppressed atom fails; [ ] no name, identifier, residential address, one person's pay or person-level row appears (PRV-129); where people live is shown no finer than the district, with no building, street or block (PRV-130); home-to-work flows are exact sums of published `home_area_share` rows from each whole home municipality of `ha_home` to the site's catchment snapped on `ha_work_count` (card C39; MTH-112, MTH-113; PRV-131), drawn with flowmap.gl and animated with deck.gl's TripsLayer as aggregate flows, never as the trail of a person or a trip, and a planted per-person path fails (document 05 SYN-59); whether that form meets the case "totals between district pairs" is the owner's question (OI-147); no employer is named beside a pool or a flow (PRV-132); a Qatari and non-Qatari split is shown only where its cell passes, otherwise it is withheld with its reason (PRV-133); [ ] the four planted violations of decision 0023 item A1 each fail: a pool cell below the minimum, a flow below the minimum, an employer named beside a pool, and a home location finer than the district (TST-246); [ ] the talent pool carries the caveat key `labour_pool_not_applicants`, the competing demand reads as jobs held in those occupations and never as vacancies or openings, nothing is worded as people available to hire or as advice to act, and no model computes or chooses a figure (rules 16 and 17); [ ] every figure comes from tested code in `eii_core.calc`, with a regression test on the hand-made fixture of card C39 whose arithmetic a reader can check (MTH-112); [ ] the claims and components of the family exist in the closed catalogues.
- Scope: the method of talent available near this site in `core/eii_core/calc/`, its tool `workers_within_reach`, the flow components under `web/`, catalogue entries and tests.
- Depends: S6-T01, S6-T07, S6-T08, S5-T10.
- Requirements: MTH-01, PUB-29, FE-51, FE-144, FE-150, DES-208, DES-211, SYN-59, MTH-112, MTH-113, MTH-122, PRV-18, PRV-30, PRV-127, PRV-128, PRV-129, PRV-130, PRV-131, PRV-132, PRV-133, DAT-165, BE-132, TST-246, TST-257, TST-258, TST-259.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.
### S6-T21 — Best three sites, a company's own branches and "how sure"
- Decision 0038 named sub-step — **exact best k sites** (inventory 19): step one audits G, R, F as defined in the decision; stop and record why if a blocking fact fails. Baseline: Existing spopt location-allocation. Pass/evidence: Cross-check p-median and maximal coverage via HiGHS against spopt with deterministic fixtures; preserve editable criteria and no verdict; settle any unspecified comparison criterion before run. A pass follows decision 0036 item 2; otherwise retain baseline and record results. Existing dependencies and gate remain; fold-in only, no new task.
- Goal: Choose the best three sites by location-allocation, show the effect on a company's own branches, and say "how sure" from Monte Carlo and Sobol runs, extending card C34, the rank-stability sentence and the `branch_check` playbook ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item A5).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] the three sites are chosen in `eii_core.calc` with PySAL `spopt`, after its current documentation and licence are read and pinned, by the model the user picks, maximal coverage or p-median with three sites, over candidates of one kind and criteria the user can see and change; set cover never chooses the sites and only reports beside them how many sites would cover all demand, or `NO_DATA` with the reason key `not_coverable` (MTH-114); a regression test on a hand-made fixture has an answer a reader can check; [ ] the Monte Carlo and Sobol runs use SALib over the distance exponents, rents, wages and catchment limits, solving the model again at each run, with a fixed seed that never forms a bare run of exactly eight or eleven digits; the wage level enters only as an eligibility ceiling on the released median pay band, and no protected figure is a demand weight (PRV-41); the result is reported only as "holds in X of 1,000 runs; most sensitive to ...", with X a Fact chip, and where the result is the same over the whole design no index is computed and no input is named (`holds_constant_in_design`; MTH-115); [ ] the rent in the runs is only the user's own rent, or the guide and recorded prices of S2-T19 labelled as prices, and a planted generated rent, or a price labelled as a rent, fails (decision 0023 item 12); [ ] the effect on the company's own branches is the user's scenario from branch locations the user gives as typed inputs, worded as shared reach, never as lost sales; the sample file of case C4 joins it through `branch_check` (S8-T01) and card C34 takes the "how sure" line when S7-T14 builds it; [ ] the rank-stability sentence of every rank of the best three sites carries the "how sure" line beside it; [ ] no output is a go/no-go verdict, a probability claim, a confidence claim or advice to act, and a planted probability wording fails (rule 25); [ ] every function is a pure function of its inputs and seed.
- Scope: the location-allocation and sensitivity modules in `core/eii_core/calc/`, the extension of the rank-stability sentence, components and tests.
- Depends: S6-T11, S6-T20.
- Requirements: MTH-01, MTH-45, MTH-57, MTH-64, MTH-65, MTH-107, FE-110, PRV-41, PRV-65, MTH-114, MTH-115, BE-133, DAT-166, FE-151, DES-213.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.

**Decision 0038 spike protocol.** Step one audits blocking facts (G: register geocodes; D: issue/cancellation dates; R: road network/speed profile; S: real-series number/length; F: go-live fields/volumes/keys). A failed audit stops that spike with its reason. Settle missing measurement contracts before execution, never guess them. Failures retain the baseline and record measured results. A pass follows decision 0036's same pipeline, invented real-shaped swap fixture, go-live re-fit/re-backtest, own interval/error procedure, fixed seeds, pinned versions/retreat pins and CPU fallback, with rule 25 and decision 0035 display honesty. Gates, controls and reviews remain. No capability is claimed built.

### S6-T29 — Spatio-temporal GNN traffic and demand spike
- Goal: Measure decision 0038 inventory item(s) 11 behind the critical path.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] step one audits R, S, F, stopping and recording why if a blocking fact fails; [ ] baseline: MLP and historical-average baselines. [ ] Compare held-out traffic/demand errors to both baselines; evaluation supplies no numerical gain threshold, so obtain a reviewed pre-run criterion. Deterministic training, CPU fallback and real-count go-live re-fit are mandatory. GPU only after measured batch-budget miss; byte-identical outputs across workers; no GPU random draw is a figure of record. [ ] failure keeps baseline and records measured results; a pass satisfies the common spike protocol above.
- Scope: C25, C27 and C33 pipeline and comparison; no real traffic feed is contacted.
- Depends: S6-T08, S6-T17, S4-T17.
- Requirements: decision 0038 items 1 to 9 and Accepted spike inventory; decision 0036 item 2; AGENTS.md rules 10, 12, 16 to 18, 25 and 42.
- Delivery: hard; builder persona principal methods architect (privacy engineer for S5); strict reviewer persona independent statistical/privacy reviewer from another maker; status queued.

### S6-T33 — Spike: catalogue enforcement (native dispatcher against A2UI and json-render)
- Goal: Measure whether an adapter adds value while preserving EII's native typed dispatcher and closed catalogues.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] write pass/fail before the run; use identical invented valid, malformed, adversarial and withheld-result fixtures for the native dispatcher, A2UI and json-render; [ ] zero unauthorised components, prose, queries, facts or actions render, with a planted violation for each category; [ ] interrupt each stream and prove that no partially validated claim appears; [ ] exercise the one validated action dispatcher shared by story, Explore and assistant, catalogue version negotiation, severity-tagged validation and user activation for side effects, planting unsupported versions, downgraded blocking errors and unactivated actions, with unknown keys/properties and wrong slots rejected, one repair then template fallback; [ ] compare a thin AG-UI adapter with plain application events by lines of code and failure modes; [ ] record either native dispatcher alone or the retained adapter and its reason; failed or blocked evidence keeps the baseline, with no threshold loosened after a run.
- Scope: isolated catalogue and transport spike, fixtures and evidence; no runtime dependency enters the critical path.
- Depends: S3-T07; accepted decision 0042 merged.
- Requirements: decision 0042 items 4, 5, 6, 8, 9 and 13; AI-35, AI-36, AI-46, AI-48, AI-49, AI-50.
- Delivery: hard; personas Frontend and AI; status queued.

### S6-T34 — Spike: charts and performance (ECharts, Observable Plot, visx)
- Goal: Measure chart integration and performance before admitting specialised chart libraries.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] write pass/fail before the run, using the same invented hourly profile, comparison small multiples and forecast range in all three libraries; [ ] measure incremental gzip and Brotli bytes, parse time and memory in the build; [ ] the owner measures frame rate and linked-update latency on the presenter laptop and a phone through a loopback page that writes a trace checked by a script, in the manner of S1-T12; the build never waits for the native run and missing evidence is not a pass; [ ] proposed pass is linked updates under 100 ms at the 95th percentile and playback above 30 frames per second, with any stricter existing budget retained and thresholds never loosened after a run; [ ] every chart has ARIA descriptions, its accessible table of the same facts and identical Western-digit numeric strings through the Fact chip family in both languages, with no model-authored specification, expression or data address; [ ] plant a missing table or description, numeral outside the chip, wrong digits, origin-encoding decal and each forbidden renderer input, and require rejection; plant malformed/missing traces and traces on the failing side of each threshold to prove the evidence checker; [ ] record results and pass/fail per library, retaining ECharts baseline when a challenger fails.
- Scope: chart spike and build measurements, native trace page and checker; no invented forecast calculation and no existing performance gate relaxed.
- Depends: S1-T12; accepted decision 0042 merged.
- Requirements: decision 0042 items 2, 8, 9 and 13; FE-39, FE-41, FE-62, FE-63, FE-82, FE-88, FE-89, TST-132.
- Delivery: hard; personas Frontend; status queued.

### S6-T06 — Review follow-up for slice S6
- Goal: Close every proposed open item that the reviews of slice S6 entered in `OPEN_ITEMS.md`, before the slice S6 gate.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] each item of the slice is closed by a fix or by one line saying why it is not valid or is out of scope under REV-42; [ ] the reviewer checks every closure; [ ] no item is deferred out of this task; [ ] no item of the slice is left open.
