**slice S6 gate.** The flagship question is answered end to end with no model call; every number resolves to a fact with a complete source chain; every receipt carries the accounting-gate result; the evidence bundle is under 1.5 seconds at the 95th percentile locally; visual tests pass at the eight named sizes in English and Arabic; a drawn area, a radius and a walk or drive time at a chosen hour each return the location evidence, with protected figures snapped with the snap sentence and Public and Simulated layers on the exact shape; presence shows estimated counts with ranges for every situation, the summer switch and the three event days, worded "estimated people around this location"; every rank and fit shows its criteria, weights, values and rank-stability sentence, with a Public-only mode, and a forecast, base-rate or estimate criterion shows its interval, range or counts, method and source; every forecast shows its empirical interval, its caption counts and its measured backtest error, a series too short returns `NO_DATA` with its reason, shown as "not enough history", and a released series with a carried-forward month in its window is not forecast and returns `CARRIED_FORWARD`; every base rate shows its numerator, denominator and period, and the Public-licence survival branch returns `NO_DATA` with `no_denominator`; the site estimate shows its low, middle and high cases, its main drivers and visible editable assumptions, with the required backend marker `estimate_on_simulated_inputs` (backend-only under decision 0035 item 3; never rendered) while an input is Simulated or Synthetic, and stays off screen while OI-03 is open; the figure of people present by pay band is returned only when every row it draws on is published; and the 3D view and the traffic provider are off by default, with every gate passing while they are off; talent available near this site shows the talent pool and the competing demand as two released figures side by side on catchments snapped to release units, qualified people within reach per competing job within reach from the two released tables when both inputs pass (MTH-122), and its home-to-work flows are exact sums of published `home_area_share` rows from each whole home municipality to the site's snapped catchment (PRV-131), with no per-person path; no talent answer shows a name, an identifier, a residential address, one person's pay, a home location finer than the district or an employer beside a pool or flow, and a pool cell below the minimum, a flow below the minimum, an employer named beside a pool and a home location finer than the district each fail as planted violations; movement aggregates may show age band, gender and nationality group splits where each cell passes (decision 0023 item 16); no drive-time catchment exceeds 30 minutes (decision 0023 item 17; TST-256); and the best three sites carry their "how sure" line and no verdict or probability, site fit shows a plot only as permitted by zoning and looking empty, never as approved or available, the site answer leads with people working here and people living here, each count beside its average, and the site estimate has no prefilled share or spend ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md); S6-T02, S6-T12, S6-T17, S6-T20, S6-T21). The conversation test lab's report over at least 500 questions is attached as evidence. TST-269, TST-270 and TST-273 meet their absolute thresholds on their declared inputs. The days 1-6 block (archive, three land-price and constraint dossiers) precedes the days 22-26 block (clock, staffing, delivery and three-site scenarios); any feed-dependent scene without validated input shows missing evidence.

## slice S7 — The other journeys and the three services

These coarse tasks are refined at the slice S6 gate without weakening their criteria.

**Priority order.** Decision 0070 item A and the slice S6 priority note above put the existing labour-shed staffing comparison, imports beside local manufacturing, second-site shared reach, parcel diligence and own-rent break-even workbench first after their dependencies, alongside the robust shortlist. The guided demo uses the same priority sequence; every existing criterion remains.

### S7-T01 — Unit economics and break-even
- Goal: Build deterministic unit economics, six-input break-even and the optional target-surplus scenario, with the agency's incentive packages as optional inputs, without forecasting success.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] the six inputs are monthly rent, seats, average bill, opening days defaulted to 30, optional expected covers per day and optional monthly staff cost; [ ] every output comes from `eii_core.calc`; [ ] invalid input fails safely; [ ] results are Inferred and tagged as user inputs; [ ] C4 derives each component's share of output from its published value divided by its group total times the publisher's percentage of that group to output, and compensation and operating-surplus shares from the published value divided by employees divided by published output per worker; the production value is neither derived nor shown; [ ] the zero-surplus regression uses `m = 0.313` and returns QAR 127,796 a month, 36 covers a day and 0.6 seat turns, with QAR 56,340 maximum rent and QAR 85.20 minimum bill at 50 expected covers; [ ] the `U = 0.197` target-surplus regression returns `m - U = 0.116`, QAR 344,827.59 a month, 96 covers a day and 1.6 seat turns, with QAR 20,880 maximum rent and QAR 229.89 minimum bill at 50 expected covers; [ ] the target case is labelled "to earn the sector's 2022 surplus share" and is never called break-even; [ ] the agency's incentive packages are optional scenario inputs, tagged "your inputs" with their source page, and never change a Public figure.
- Scope: C4 calculation, tool facts, break-even component and tests.
- Depends: S6-T05, S2-T12, S2-T11, S2-T19, S2-T28, S4-T09.
- Amendment ([decision 0045](docs/decisions/0045-eiis-own-capabilities-and-the-snapshot-archive.md) item 5; capabilities 5 and L6): [ ] break-even covers come from tested code over the visible inputs, with the district commercial guide price shown as dated land-cost context and the user's own rent as the only rent; a land purchase price is never converted into a monthly financing cost without the user's explicit financing inputs; [ ] "what would change your answer" runs a one-at-a-time, code-computed input swing to the next break-even breakpoint and names the single assumption whose cheapest next test crosses it, with its current value, breakpoint and direction shown and no advice or success claim; a planted model-selected assumption, a planted prefilled rent, a planted guide price labelled as rent or a deal price, and a planted hand-written breakpoint each fail (MTH-25, MTH-26, MTH-61, MTH-62, MTH-116, MTH-117).
- Amendment ([decision 0047](docs/decisions/0047-eiis-own-decision-backlog-and-acceptance-targets.md) item A4): [ ] with the user's room count, rate, occupancy and costs, dated `hotel_ext` room-supply and rate context, and the S6-T14 backtest-bounded forecast or its refusal, tested code computes a star-class room-yield scenario and shows room-supply dilution as a separate scenario driver; a planted hand-written yield, forecast without its interval/error, invented occupancy or sale price used as a room rate fails.
- Amendment (decision 0047 item A5): [ ] with `survey_ext` activity accounts, `wage_sector` and Public price indices beside the user's menu prices, volumes and costs, tested code computes restaurant margin, covers and cost breakpoints at matched activity and period; a planted mismatched-period blend, model-computed margin, invented restaurant production value or index treated as the user's actual cost fails.
- Amendment (decision 0047 item A10): [ ] with one chosen S2-T12 incentive package, its dated application-window source and the user's cost scenario, tested code shows the same scenario before and after only eligible spend, lists every unmet condition and leaves an unverified window as missing evidence; a planted ineligible-cost subsidy, omitted condition, undated open-window claim or approval/success forecast fails.
- Amendment (decision 0047 item A22): [ ] with S4-T09 weather profiles, Public utility context and the user's tariffs, equipment and cooling load, tested code computes summer cooling headroom and operating-cost scenarios at matched geography and period; a planted invented utility capacity or tariff, weather value converted into measured consumption, negative-headroom success verdict or model-written cost fails.
- Requirements: MTH-25, MTH-26, DES-110, MTH-03, MTH-06, FE-44, MTH-61, MTH-62, DAT-143, AI-111, MTH-76, MTH-97, MTH-98, PUB-85, PUB-90, PUB-105, DAT-141, DAT-144, SYN-52, SYN-54.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.

### S7-T10 — Where to grow (journey D6, service S2)
- Goal: Show openings and closures over time on the map, cluster change and the construction pipeline, and compare growth and operating options (a new branch, a larger site, a new territory, a revised service model) with the barriers to resolve.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] public licence cancellations, and issues where document 04 records the series, stay on their official geography as Public facts, with bulk months flagged; [ ] synthetic openings and closures by area and month, while they stay (open under [decision 0022](docs/decisions/0022-real-register-companies-with-a-synthetic-world.md): document 05 Q12), are marked Synthetic; [ ] the entry and closure base rates come from card C31 (S6-T15), by activity and on the source's own geography, as flows over the published active stock of the same activity and geography, labelled approximate, each with its two counts and period, and the Public-licence survival branch is `NO_DATA` with the reason key `no_denominator` and no survival figure is shown (OI-97); [ ] no function computes issued minus cancelled licences or a net-formation figure from the public tables, and nothing is computed from the Synthetic births and closures; [ ] the forecasts of card C30 for licence issues and licence cancellations and for the construction pipeline, turned into expected new households and workers by municipality, appear as separate labelled cards with their interval and measured backtest error; [ ] the night-time radiance of S3-T14 may stand beside the openings and closures as context, worded as night-time radiance and not as a measure of business activity; [ ] building permits and completion certificates show the construction pipeline by municipality with their latest year; [ ] the option comparison uses the second-site comparison of S7-T05 and lists the barriers to resolve, never a verdict, a forecast of the venture's success or advice to act; [ ] property prices show their deal counts; [ ] where FDI projects inform the comparison, FDI projects are shown in full, with every field available on cards, lists and the map; each card, list and map legend carries collapsed credits naming the agency as publisher, the data gatherer as the page names it, the date read and a link to the dashboard, with the page's caveat that the data "includes estimates of publicly announced projects" and "does not capture all investment projects"; capital and jobs are labelled as announced estimates, never added to measured figures or shown beside them without that label, never as a lookup of a firm; [ ] the answer comes through the journey's declarative playbook, with no model call.
- Amendment (decision 0036): the existing Public-licence branch retains its missing-cohort refusal; S6-T23 and S6-T24 add source-general survival and entry methods under MTH-123. Generated provenance is not itself a failing test.
- Scope: growth methods, option comparison, playbook, components and tests.

### S7-T14 — Catchment-share scenario
- Goal: Build the catchment-share scenario that joins the break-even calculator: customers choose between the user's outlet and the real competitors from public listings.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] customers choose between the user's outlet and the competitors from public listings by distance and by an attractiveness that the user sets for the user's outlet against the competitors as a group, or by competitor category, and never for one named business; [ ] the scenario shows the share of the user's outlet and the share of the competitors, split by competitor category and by distance band (for example Italian restaurants within 500 m; other restaurants), never per named business; [ ] a cell of that split that holds a single competitor is merged into the next wider cell (the same category in the next distance band, then "other"); [ ] where a single competitor is still left after those merges, no split is shown, only the share of the user's outlet and the share of the competitors together; with one competitor in all, only the share of the user's outlet is shown and no share is stated for the competitors, each terminal case with its own regression test; [ ] the categories come from the reviewed category mapping of the places layer (S2-T15) and the distance bands are a closed list of the card (`ASSUMPTION`: within 500 m, 500 m to 1 km, 1 km to 2 km, beyond); [ ] a named competitor keeps only its Public facts and its distance, and no share, estimate or score is computed for it for display, shown, stored or logged; [ ] every assumption is visible and editable, the typed values come from the closed list of scenario inputs extended for this card (OI-33), and the result is Inferred and tagged as the user's inputs; [ ] the result is the user's scenario (caveat key `scenario_not_forecast`), is never called a forecast and is never worded as lost sales; [ ] every output comes from `eii_core.calc`, invalid input fails safely, and a regression test has arithmetic that a reader can check by hand.
- Scope: calculation card C34, the scenario tool, components and tests.
- Depends: S7-T01, S7-T05, S3-T05, S6-T21.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) item A5): [ ] the card takes the "how sure" line of S6-T21 beside its result, from the runs of card C40 through `location_allocation` for a site that tool returned among its three sites in the same answer; `catchment_scenario` runs no runs of its own, X and N are Fact chips, and the line is never a percentage, a probability or a confidence (BE-124, FE-132, MTH-115).
- Requirements: MTH-25, MTH-26, MTH-57, PRV-65, FE-44, DES-110, MTH-107, DAT-162, AI-123.
- Delivery: hard; personas Methods; status queued.

Existing dependencies and gate remain; fold-in only, no new task.

**Decision 0038 spike protocol.** Step one audits blocking facts (G: register geocodes; D: issue/cancellation dates; R: road network/speed profile; S: real-series number/length; F: go-live fields/volumes/keys). A failed audit stops that spike with its reason. Settle missing measurement contracts before execution, never guess them. Failures retain the baseline and record measured results. A pass follows decision 0036's same pipeline, invented real-shaped swap fixture, go-live re-fit/re-backtest, own interval/error procedure, fixed seeds, pinned versions/retreat pins and CPU fallback, with rule 25 and decision 0035 display honesty. Gates, controls and reviews remain. No capability is claimed built.

- Scope: C12 and C16 service-catchment calculations, Public table contracts, components, playbook entries and tests. No new source assertion, synthetic record, privacy parameter or protected-table aggregation.
- Depends: S6-T05, S6-T13, S3-T12, S3-T14, S2-T11, S2-T19, S2-T28, S2-T29.
- Requirements: C12, C16, C38; MTH-01, MTH-06, MTH-45, MTH-46, MTH-57, MTH-111; PUB-01, PUB-04, PUB-16, PUB-82, PUB-85, PUB-86, PUB-90, PUB-99, PUB-105; PRV-09, PRV-122; BE-119; FE-153; TST-216, TST-240; decision 0047 item A for verified school seats.
- Delivery: Deep; reason: the task computes and releases new numbers (service-catchment shortlists, whole-cell population sums and reach sets) on audience surfaces; it changes no ingress or privacy control. **slice S7 gate.** Each method reproduces its public worked example; no regression value is left unpinned; each card passes the provenance audit; each of journeys D1 and D3 to D7 ends with its own answer end to end with no model call, and the three services are produced as journey outputs; register companies appear only with their card fields and employee counts, ordered by tested code over criteria the user can see and change and never by a company's wages or another unreleased value, and a company's wages and other labour and residence details appear only as released aggregates ([decision 0022](docs/decisions/0022-real-register-companies-with-a-synthetic-world.md)); and no answer forecasts the success of a venture or estimates anything for a named real business or competitor; a forecast, estimate or base rate appears only as the output of tested code with its interval, range or counts, method and source; a scenario is the user's and is never called a forecast; and no answer gives a verdict, a probability, a confidence claim or advice to act. The demonstration evidence orders days 7-11 (hotel and restaurant), days 12-16 (manufacturing, supplier and tender) and days 17-21 (construction, health and training), each with its next fact to investigate and missing evidence where a feed is unvalidated; S7-T24's health, school and age-specific service catchments pass their planted guards.

## slice S8 — The four cases and the assistant

These coarse tasks are refined at the slice S7 gate without weakening their criteria.

### S8-T01 — Deterministic router and playbooks
- Decision 0038 named sub-step — **catalogue retrieval** (inventory 15; R4): step one audits F as defined in the decision; stop and record why if a blocking fact fails. Baseline: Typed parser and clarification card. Pass/evidence: Use S6-T30's in-process index only to order closed keys; router misses on golden/exceed sets must drop or keep baseline. No pgvector without its own decision and preflight change. A pass follows decision 0036 item 2; otherwise retain baseline and record results. Existing dependencies and gate remain; fold-in only, no new task.
- Goal: Route to the closed, declarative playbooks of every journey before any provider call.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] bilingual prepared questions route deterministically to the playbook of every journey; [ ] closed values constrain user numbers; [ ] each playbook runs without a model; [ ] the playbook list is closed and versioned; [ ] questions about a listed series, a base rate, a site estimate or a scenario route to their families (`series_forecast`, `base_rates`, `site_estimate`, `catchment_scenario`, `revenue_range_scenario`) and are not declined.
- Scope: deterministic router, playbook registry and unit tests.
- Depends: S7-T13.
- Amendment ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md) items 11, A4 and A5): [ ] the default selection of `site_check` follows the site answer's order, with the age profile as its own Public component after the two blocks, and both blocks on the requested headline area, the area the user picked (a drawn shape, a radius, a walk time or a drive time; walk 5 when nothing is picked); with a drive time picked both blocks, the working block's main sectors and employer sizes from the `register` block included, use that drive area's identifier and never walk 5, presence by hour and the public context are read on that same drive area, outlets with neighbours, the people present by pay band and the site estimate stay on the near area, and no third area is made; a planted substitution of walk 5 in either block, in its `register` figures, in presence by hour or in the public context fails (AI-131, TST-250; document 08 section 7); `branch_check` adds the best three sites and "how sure" of S6-T21 over the branches of the sample file; a confirmed voice transcript routes exactly as typed text (AI-128).

**Decision 0038 spike protocol.** Step one audits blocking facts (G: register geocodes; D: issue/cancellation dates; R: road network/speed profile; S: real-series number/length; F: go-live fields/volumes/keys). A failed audit stops that spike with its reason. Settle missing measurement contracts before execution, never guess them. Failures retain the baseline and record measured results. A pass follows decision 0036's same pipeline, invented real-shaped swap fixture, go-live re-fit/re-backtest, own interval/error procedure, fixed seeds, pinned versions/retreat pins and CPU fallback, with rule 25 and decision 0035 display honesty. Gates, controls and reviews remain. No capability is claimed built.

### S8-T18 — The own-data overlay with a "nothing sent" receipt
- Goal: Add a narrow company-owned CSV exception to the no-browser-file rule, overlaying branch or delivery evidence in Explore and the guided story while keeping every row inside the local session and proving that no firm row or derived value reaches a model or an external destination.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] a file input and drop/paste control exist only on the own-data overlay, accept only the existing closed branch and delivery CSV schemas of S8-T08, and submit the CSV body only to the local same-origin session route; the closed-key sample-file route and every other page remain unchanged, and a file body, path or URL sent to any other route is refused; [ ] unknown or missing columns, malformed rows, a formula-like cell, a personal-name or personal-identifier field, a real registration or establishment identifier, and an unexpected file type are refused before a fact or map feature is created, with no row retained; accepted rows bind to the current session and seat in API-process memory only; [ ] the route is disabled in remote mode and the browser makes no external request; no CSV row, coordinate, derived fact, brief or map feature is written to a release, database or other store, local or session storage, cache, log, ledger beyond call/status, memo, egress record, backup or freeze bundle, and no brief containing one can be saved; [ ] Reset, seat change, session end and process restart delete the overlay, and a second seat or session can read neither its rows nor its derived facts; [ ] only fixed application read functions and `eii_core.calc` compute overlay facts, every numeral renders through a Fact chip with the "your inputs" tag and complete method lineage, no model receives a row or derived value, and no audience origin label or persistent illustrative-scenario notice appears while backend origin and generator metadata remain; [ ] the ephemeral "nothing sent" receipt states precisely that no firm row left the local API process and no firm row or derived value was sent to a model or an external destination, while authorised derived facts may reach the local browser; it reports the actual route, model-payload, egress and persistence checks; it is backed by an exact-payload egress scan and post-session store/log/memo scans, is not a signed file or a formal privacy guarantee, and disappears with the session; [ ] Explore and the guided story share the same validated overlay dispatcher and session state, with keyboard and non-drag alternatives, bilingual identical fact strings and offline operation; [ ] plant a file control on another page, an unknown column, malformed row, personal field, forbidden identifier, cross-route body, remote-mode upload, external request, model-payload row or derived value, saved brief, persistent row or fact, cross-seat read, surviving reset/restart overlay, unchipped numeral, audience origin label, false receipt and dispatcher bypass; each guard has its own failing test before the passing case, and a check that cannot inspect the exact payload or persistence targets cannot issue a passing receipt.
- Scope: the narrow own-data file controls and local session route, schema validation, in-memory overlay adapter and deterministic read functions, Explore/story integration, ephemeral receipt, catalogue entries and focused security, privacy, bilingual and interaction tests; the corresponding narrow amendments to ARC-128, DAT-149 and DAT-150, BE-109 and BE-118, FE-120 and their no-file scans; no new source, model tool, persistent receipt or remote upload path.
- Depends: S8-T01, S8-T02, S8-T03, S8-T08, S8-T15, S6-T37, S6-T39.
- Requirements: decision 0045 item 6 and capability 13 of its mapping; ARC-73, ARC-104, ARC-128, DAT-149, DAT-150, BE-109, BE-118, FE-120, AI-46, AI-50, PRV-89; AGENTS.md rules 10, 12, 16 to 25 and 37.
- Delivery: Deep (it creates a controlled real-company-data ingress exception and must prove session, seat, egress and persistence boundaries); personas: senior product-security engineer (builder) with strict principal privacy and adversarial reviewer, and senior bilingual product engineer (builder) with strict principal design and accessibility reviewer; status queued.

### S8-T19 — Typed reference to an earlier answer item in the chain slot
- Goal: Extend AI-17's typed context with a reference to an item of an earlier answer, without carrying earlier model prose forward (decision 0070 item C).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] (0070 C) define a closed, versioned chain-slot reference contract and update document 09 AI-17 and the owning data contracts, with the application offering and resolving only permitted earlier item references; planted unknown property, invented item and earlier prose payload each refuse; [ ] (0070 C) a follow-up fixture selects a specific earlier item, resolves it to typed context, runs the existing closed tools for current facts and validates the new answer through the same catalogues/accounting gate, with planted reuse of an old answer as fact, stale release/catalogue and unsupported fact each refused; [ ] (0070 C) wrong-seat, wrong-session, unavailable and suppressed references disclose neither the old item nor a hidden value, with a planted case for each; [ ] (0070 C) memo and replay bind to the resolved typed reference and current authorised context, retaining all existing no-memo and session-only input exclusions, with planted memo collision, saved own-data reference and cross-seat replay refused; [ ] (0070 C) deterministic template and recorded-provider fixtures cover English and Arabic offline, each question still starts a fresh model conversation, all numerals remain Fact chips and every guard has recorded red/green evidence.
- Scope: existing typed context/chain-slot and router projection, brief validator, memo/replay binding and focused tests, with task-owned amendments to document 09 and data contracts. Slice S8 owns these layers through S8-T01, S8-T03 and S8-T06; no transcript memory, new data source, raw earlier response or model-written screen text.
- Depends: S8-T01, S8-T03, S8-T06.
- Requirements: [decision 0070](docs/decisions/0070-the-decision-pack-of-the-value-studies.md) item C; document 09 AI-17, AI-14, AI-36, AI-42, AI-63, AI-67 and AI-69; AGENTS.md rules 7, 18 to 23 and 37.
- Delivery: hard; personas AI and Backend; status queued.

**slice S8 gate.** The golden set passes five out of five runs on every prepared question; the must-decline set passes at 100%; 500 distinct structured briefs from the template-writer and recorded-provider fixtures validate against the catalogues before repair or fallback, with every fallback counted and reported; the injection suite passes with zero successes; all build gates run against the recorded provider with no key or network, OI-01 stays open, and live-model runs (prepared recordings refreshed live, model-returned briefs, a full live run) are enabled only by an owner decision at go-live; and each of the four cases starts with one click and runs end to end with no model call, with each sample file inside the session that loaded it; and a question asked aloud runs only after its transcript is confirmed, the voice speaks only template-writer text, no audio leaves the machine, and every gate passes with the speech models absent ([decision 0023](docs/decisions/0023-ten-additions-and-the-data-rule.md); S8-T10). The conversation test lab's report over at least 1,000 questions is attached as evidence. Its golden-scenario run names the Qatari-day journey, three sites and what would change your answer, staffing geography with the privacy snap, and the own-data overlay with its "nothing sent" receipt.

### S10-T01 — Signed attack replay
- Goal: Revalidate prepared attack recordings at start-up, serve the complement step through `POST /api/proof/scene` (PRV-119) and refuse tampered evidence. This task cites that route and does not restate its schema.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] the complement step calls `POST /api/proof/scene` (PRV-119), where one manifest-listed fixture id selects two prepared requests that differ by one published merged unit of at least two base cells, and no figure exists for any member base cell alone; [ ] the endpoint returns their pre-validated unit sets; [ ] production tools accept no cell or unit list, and every protected figure still snaps to whole published release units whatever shape is requested; [ ] the scene separately shows the typed phrase declined by the application; [ ] the injection step rejects invalid structured output, rejects its one prepared invalid repair, falls back to the playbook and logs from signed fixture identity; [ ] the unprotected comparison is a labelled replay of signed build-time results and names any canary only by its scenario label; [ ] tools reproduce every prepared fact; [ ] changed fact, release, prompt or brief fails; [ ] no controls-off path exists at run time.
- Scope: `POST /api/proof/scene`, typed-phrase decline fixture, signed injection fixture, labelled unprotected replay, replay validator, signed attack fixtures and negative tests.
- Depends: S9-T05.

## slice S11 — Hardening and hand-over

These coarse tasks are refined at the slice S10 gate without weakening their criteria.

### S11-T01 — Command and freeze hardening
- Goal: Harden preflight, database, start, stop and both no-flag `./eii freeze` runs for the stage machine.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] preflight reports every check; [ ] only `db-up` starts the database; [ ] wrong release receipt refuses start; [ ] the first freeze run requires current green evidence and a recorded human review of the release (role, date, and that the survival report and the signed attack report were read); [ ] no review record, no freeze; [ ] the first run writes the data bundle, checksum list and pending `freeze.json` and appends the register line; [ ] the second no-flag run starts from a base-branch commit containing that line, builds and verifies the application archive, writes its checksum beside the bundle and marks `freeze.json` complete; [ ] for a frozen release only, the installer and `./eii up` exit 7 unless the installed application's register contains the line and `freeze.json` says `complete`; [ ] a build release with no `freeze.json` is not checked.
- Scope: command implementations, health checks, freeze builder and operational tests.
- Depends: S10-T05.
- Requirements: DAT-133, OPS-06, OPS-27, OPS-32, PLN-20, RUN-12, RUN-13.
- Delivery: hard; personas Platform; status queued.

### S11-T06 — Export register
- Goal: Append one export-register line on the first `./eii freeze` run, enforce the two uniqueness rules, and complete the frozen bundle on the second no-flag run.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] the first freeze run appends `window_key`, `protected_sha256`, `world_seed`, `rules_version_id`, `parameter_set`, `release_id` and `code_version`, in that order, as one line of `infra/export_register.jsonl`; [ ] continuous integration checks the register file alone, allows one `protected_sha256` per `window_key` and one `window_key` per `world_seed`, and fails if an existing line is changed or removed; [ ] a planted changed line, a planted deleted line, a second `protected_sha256` for one `window_key` and a second `window_key` for one `world_seed` all fail; [ ] a register conflict refuses freeze, and any post-freeze change to rules version, exported set, treatment code or world generator requires a new `world_seed` and a new build before another freeze (PRV-120); [ ] the second no-flag run from the base-branch commit containing the line builds and verifies the application archive, writes its checksum beside the bundle and marks `freeze.json` complete; [ ] a frozen bundle is refused at install or start with exit 7 when its line is missing from the installed application's register or `freeze.json` is incomplete, and the remedy is to finish the two-run freeze rather than change the seed; [ ] no signed receipt file is created.
- Scope: both freeze runs, `freeze.json`, the application archive and checksum, `infra/export_register.jsonl`, the frozen-bundle stage-machine checks and the continuous-integration register check.
- Depends: S11-T01, S5-T07.
- Requirements: PRV-120, RUN-13.
- Delivery: hard; personas Platform; status queued.

### S11-T09 — Protected-release freeze and promotion operations
- Goal: Extend S3-T28's live/staging operations to protected releases using the existing two-run freeze and export-register contracts of S11-T01 and S11-T06 (decision 0071 items 5 and 6; L-1219).
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] (0071 items 5 and 6) integrate and document `./eii freeze` twice with no new flag: the first run requires current green evidence and the recorded human review, writes the bundle, checksum list and pending `freeze.json`, and appends exactly one matching export-register line; missing review, unread report, wrong manifest value, missing bundle file and extra mode flag are separately planted and refused; [ ] (0071 items 5 and 6) after normal merge of that line, the second no-flag run from the containing base-branch commit builds and verifies the application archive offline, writes its checksum, marks `freeze.json` complete and appends no line; planted unmerged-line, wrong-commit, failed-offline-install, wrong-checksum, premature-complete and duplicate-line cases each fail; [ ] (0071 item 6) rule 12's canonical `window_key` and register immutability and uniqueness remain enforced by S11-T06, including the new seed and build required after a post-freeze rules-version, exported-set, treatment-code or world-generator change; changed-window, changed-line, deleted-line, conflicting-protected-hash, conflicting-seed-to-window and each changed-input-without-new-seed/build plant refuse freeze, and a planted signed receipt never substitutes for the register; [ ] (0071 items 5 and 6) installation and start of a frozen protected release each exit 7 when the installed application's register lacks the matching line or `freeze.json` is incomplete, with separate missing-line and incomplete-descriptor plants at both boundaries; finish the two-run freeze as the remedy, and plant a seed-change workaround to prove it cannot satisfy these checks; [ ] (0071 items 2 to 5) prove the protected build/load/freeze/install/start, staging, promotion and rollback path using S3-T28's isolated environments and complete L-1212 checklist as stated in decision 0071 item 3; plant each missing/failed checklist result, wrong target, missing promotion log/result, cross-environment write and removed rollback folder separately and prove refusal, then record the green local path with its release id and checklist results; [ ] (0071 items 5 and 6) retain the `public_only` path unchanged: it is never frozen or registered and has none of the protected-window fields, and the frozen-only exit-7 install/start test is not imposed on it; planted public-only freeze/register entry, dummy protected data and wrongly applied frozen-only startup check fail the tests (DAT-133, OPS-63, ARC-41); [ ] (0071 item 6) no S11-T01/S11-T06 criterion, rule-12 invariant, gate, security check, privacy attack or tolerance is weakened, both stores load with matching complete receipt and parity without fallback, no product custodian import or vault read is allowed, and offline tests use invented local fixtures with no tunnel, key or network; plant each boundary bypass and fail it, keeping rule-24 and pinned command behaviour checks.
- Scope: protected-release implementation integration split from S3-T28, its operational runbook in document 13, protected build/load through existing contracts, the two no-flag freeze runs, export-register and archive verification, frozen install/start refusal, isolated staging and protected promotion/rollback evidence and offline tests. Reuse S11-T01/S11-T06's implementations and all acceptance checks; add no alternate freeze contract, signed receipt or public-only freeze. Only the lead operates a real public route.
- Depends: S11-T06, S3-T28.
- Requirements: [decision 0071](docs/decisions/0071-after-go-live-operations-and-continuous-promotion.md) items 2 to 6; S11-T01 and S11-T06 acceptance contracts; document 03 DAT-133; document 13 OPS-58, OPS-62, OPS-63 and OPS-64; document 02 ARC-41 and ARC-22; document 06 PRV-120; AGENTS.md rules 11, 12, 24, 33, 36, 37, 44 and 45.
- Delivery: hard; personas Platform and Operations; status queued.

### S11-T07 — Review follow-up for slice S11
- Goal: Close every proposed open item that the reviews of slice S11 entered in `OPEN_ITEMS.md`, before the slice S11 gate.
- Acceptance criteria: [ ] each item of the slice is closed by a fix or by one line saying why it is not valid or is out of scope under REV-42; [ ] the reviewer checks every closure; [ ] no item is deferred out of this task; [ ] no item of the slice is left open.
- Scope: the files the closures change, and `OPEN_ITEMS.md`.
- Depends: every other task of slice S11.
- Requirements: REV-41, TST-168.
- Delivery: routine; personas as the items require; status queued.

**slice S11 gate.** Three rehearsals are complete, including one with PostgreSQL stopped and one offline; versions are frozen; the freeze carries a recorded human review of the release and passes the export-register check; the independent reviewer returns `APPROVED`; and the owner records go or no-go.
